Beverage-AI Radar: all 592 tracked companies

A source-cited landscape of who is applying AI, machine learning and data analytics across beer, whiskey and wine, plus the wider food and beverage majors and data platforms working alongside them. Every entry cites its evidence. Companies that make no AI claim are recorded and labelled as such rather than inflated, because an unchecked claim would make the whole database worthless. Last updated 2026-08-09.

Interactive dashboard · Raw JSON · llms.txt

Beer (158)

ABE Beverage Equipment (American Beer Equipment)

beer · United States · Process & operations · none · active abeequipment.com

brewhouse assisted brewing automation

ABE Beverage Equipment, formerly American Beer Equipment and a division of Norland International, builds brewhouses, fermentation, grain handling and canning lines for craft breweries. Its named control offering is the Brewhouse Automation Plus assisted brewing package, standard on new brewhouses, which automates liquor blending, vorlauf and wort grant, sparge control, lauter to kettle and whirlpool transfers, knockout to fermenter at a regulated pitch temperature, and liquor tank management with volumetric auto-stop and remote viewing and control. BrewPilot is its entry-level system for nano and brewpub scale. ABE deliberately uses off-the-shelf automation components and makes no AI or machine learning claim.

Provenance: Scout: scout:equipment. Last seen 2026-08-01.

  • https://abeequipment.com/products/brewhouses/assisted-brewing/
  • https://vigoltd.com/products/brewpilot-from-abe-beverage-equipment

Aber Instruments

beer · United Kingdom · Process & operations · none · active aberinstruments.com

inline yeast biomass measurement for pitching control

Aber Instruments makes radio frequency impedance biomass sensors that measure live yeast concentration inline during pitching and cropping, sold to breweries as the COMPACT inline system, the portable Perfect Pitch unit and the Countstar image analysis cell counter. It is a measurement and control vendor, not an analytics vendor: the site makes no machine learning or predictive claim, and the value proposition is replacing offline cell counts with a direct live-cell signal. The company is employee owned and names Heineken, SABMiller, AB InBev, Royal Unibrew, Meantime, Marble and Summit among its brewing users.

Provenance: Scout: scout:industrial. Last seen 2026-08-05.

  • https://www.aberinstruments.com/
  • https://www.aberinstruments.com/applications/brewing/

Agaram InfoTech (AgES Brewery ERP)

beer · India · Other · none · active agaraminfotech.com

Agaram InfoTech is a Chennai ERP and IT solutions company whose AgES Brewery ERP is a purpose-built brewery management system for the Indian market, one of very few Indian-domiciled brewery ERP vendors. Modules cover material management, brewing, fermentation, bright beer tracking, production planning, quality control, sales and distribution and plant maintenance, with automated excise duty calculation, batch traceability and keg tracking as the local differentiators. It is sold to craft breweries, contract brewers and regional operations, and the company claims over 100 clients across its product range. It makes no AI claim: the product offers real-time dashboards and production reporting only.

Provenance: Scout: scout:erp. Last seen 2026-08-04.

  • https://www.agaraminfotech.com/about-us
  • https://www.agaraminfotech.com/brewery-management-software

Amanda Dobbyn

beer · United States · Market data & valuation · shipping · active

data analytics / BI / market intelligence for beverage

Built a public clustering and classification analysis of 45,000-plus beers to test whether beer styles hold up statistically, a well-known beer data science project.

Provenance: Hand-verified. Last seen 2026-07-22.

Anderson-Negele (Negele Messtechnik GmbH)

beer · Germany · Process & operations · none · active anderson-negele.com

none claimed, hygienic process instrumentation

Anderson-Negele makes hygienic process instrumentation for dairy, food, beverage, pharma and life sciences, under a Hygienic by Design philosophy aligned to EHEDG, FDA and 3-A standards. Its sensors for temperature, level, flow, pressure, conductivity and turbidity are the measurement layer feeding brewery and beverage plant control systems, and the German entity Negele Messtechnik exhibits at BrauBeviale. It is a sensor vendor: the site makes no AI or machine-learning claim.

Provenance: Scout: scout:tradeshow_de. Last seen 2026-08-05.

  • https://www.anderson-negele.com
  • https://www.braubeviale.com/de-DE/aussteller-und-produkte/

Anheuser-Busch InBev

beer · Belgium · Quality & inspection · shipping · active ab-inbev.com

quality control / computer vision

AB InBev applied machine learning (Google TensorFlow, via Pluto7) to filtration sensor data at its Newark brewery, increasing barrelage per filter run by about 60 percent while stabilizing beer taste. Its Beck's brand also released Autonomous, a beer with a recipe and branding generated by AI. Its current flagship digital asset is BEES, a proprietary B2B platform that has digitized its relationship with millions of retailers worldwide and is now offered to partners; AB InBev states no AI or machine-learning claim for BEES itself, so it is recorded here as digitization and route-to-market data, not as an AI product.

Provenance: Hand-verified. Last seen 2026-08-01.

  • https://cloud.google.com/customers/abinbev-pluto7
  • https://www.ab-inbev.com/about-us/what-we-do/innovation
  • https://www.thedrinksbusiness.com/2023/03/ab-inbev-launches-ai-beer-named-becks-autonomous/

Anton van den Hengel

beer · Australia · Sensory & recipe · shipping · active

applied machine learning for beverage recipe and consumer feedback modelling

Computer vision and machine learning professor, founding director of the Australian Institute for Machine Learning, listed as Director Applied AI on Deep Liquid's team page. Deep Liquid's model was trained on over 200,000 beer recipes with input from Barossa Valley Brewing.

Provenance: Hand-verified. Last seen 2026-08-04.

Aquacycl

beer · United States · Other · none · active aquacycl.com

remote real time control of modular wastewater reactors

Aquacycl treats ultra high strength food and beverage wastewater at source with BETT, a modular bioelectrochemical reactor using electrochemically active bacteria, avoiding dilution and cutting sludge. Every reactor is remotely monitored and controlled in real time from Aquacycl, which removes the need for a full time onsite operator, and probe data is used to read microbial colony health. Its own brewery case study documents 99 percent BOD and 85 percent TSS removal at Joshua Tree Brewery, and PepsiCo is named on the site; the company has also worked through AB InBev's 100+ Accelerator. No machine learning claim is made: this is instrumented process control, not modelling.

Provenance: Scout: scout:esg. Last seen 2026-08-01.

  • https://aquacycl.com/
  • https://aquacycl.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/MBAA-TQ-Sustainable-Approach-to-Brewery-Wastewater.pdf

Arryved

beer · unknown · Process & operations · shipping · active arryved.com

taproom/point-of-sale data & analytics for craft beverage operators

Arryved is a point-of-sale and operations platform built specifically for craft beverage businesses such as breweries, taprooms, cideries and distilleries. It unifies service, online ordering, production, payments and reporting into a single data model that delivers real-time, brewery-specific analytics. Its aggregate POS dataset spans more than 2,000 taprooms and is used, with the Brewers Association, to analyze industry taproom sales trends.

Provenance: Hand-verified. Last seen 2026-07-22.

  • https://arryved.com/platform/arryved-overview/
  • https://arryved.com/products/brewery-management/
  • https://www.arryved.com/products/

Augury

beer · United States · Process & operations · shipping · active augury.com

predictive machine health on brewery packaging lines

Augury sells continuous machine-health monitoring: wireless vibration, temperature and magnetic sensors on rotating equipment feeding ML diagnostic models that classify developing faults and prescribe repairs. Heineken is named as a customer on Augury's own beverage industry page, and Augury publishes a brewery case where an alert on a filler and seamer malfunction avoided the loss of roughly 960,000 cans of beer and eight-plus hours of line downtime, plus a Top 10 global brewing company quoted on servo motor defects. This is genuine supervised ML on sensor signals, not marketing framing, though it is asset reliability rather than anything about the beer recipe or fermentation itself.

Provenance: Scout: scout:industrial. Last seen 2026-07-26.

  • http://www.augury.com/blog/machine-health/save-the-beer-save-the-day-utilizing-machine-health-in-the-beverage-industry/
  • http://www.augury.com/industries/beverage

BBULL Technology GmbH

beer · Germany · Quality & inspection · none · active bbull.com

none claimed, inspection and track and trace on beverage packaging lines

BBULL Technology supplies inspection and rejection equipment plus a track and trace system for beverage packaging lines, covering glass bottles, beverage cans, one-way and refillable PET bottles, food cans, jars and cups. The track and trace product is the data-relevant part of the range: serialised code application and reading across the line for quality and conformity records. The company is part of the BBULL group, which also includes BBULL Computer. It makes no AI or machine learning claim. Exhibited at BrauBeviale and drinktec.

Provenance: Scout: scout:tradeshow_de. Last seen 2026-08-04.

  • https://www.bbull.com
  • https://www.bbull.com/en/about-bbull-technology/about-us/
  • https://www.braubeviale.com/de-DE/aussteller-und-produkte/

BEERSOFT

beer · Canada · Process & operations · shipping · active beersoft.ca

brewery customer service and operations agents

BEERSOFT builds custom AI and agent implementations specifically for breweries, taprooms, brewpubs and beverage brands across the US and Canada. Products include RAG systems grounded in brewery documents such as menus and policies, customer service chatbots answering taproom hours, beer availability, events and reservations, and autonomous agents that process reservations and generate reports. It integrates with brewery systems including Square, Toast and Ekos. AWS Bedrock is one of the named deployment platforms alongside OpenAI API, LangChain and Azure AI, chosen for VPC integration and enterprise security. Offices listed in Fredericton, Saint John and North Vancouver.

Provenance: Scout: scout:aws. Last seen 2026-07-26.

  • https://www.beersoft.ca/ai-solutions/ai-and-agent-solutions

Bar Analytics

beer · United Kingdom · Quality & inspection · shipping · active baranalytics.co.uk

beer sales and quality analytics with AI insights

Bar Analytics runs a beer analytics platform that provides real-time sales and quality data with AI insights on beer performance globally.

Bar.on

beer · Belgium · Sensory & recipe · pilot · active baron.bar

flavour compound modelling for on demand beer formulation

Bar.on built the One Tap, a molecular beer dispenser that constructs blond, brown, IPA, tripel and non alcoholic styles in seconds by combining flavour compounds with tap water, with alcohol level, bitterness, fruitiness, sweetness and spice adjustable per pour. The formulation work is built on the flavour compound dataset assembled over a decade by professor Kevin Verstrepen's VIB and KU Leuven lab, the same body of work behind the published Belgian beer flavour prediction models. Bar.on itself markets the dispenser and personalisation rather than a machine learning product, so treat the AI here as upstream research rather than a shipped model. Founded by CEO Dirk Standaert and CPO Valentijn Destoop, raised EUR 1.8 million seed in 2022.

Provenance: Scout: scout:beer. Last seen 2026-08-01.

  • https://baron.bar/
  • https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/the-one-tap-uses-highly-innovative-molecular-recipes-and-dispensing-technology-to-combine-flavour-compounds-and-create-beer-that-caters-to-all-tastes-starting-with-water-from-the-tap-301788386.html

BarthHaas

beer · Germany · Other · none · active barthhaas.com

no substantiated ai claim

BarthHaas is the world's largest hop merchant and processor, founded in Nuremberg in 1794, supplying pellets, extracts and advanced hop products to brewers and running the BarthHaas Academy and the annual BarthHaas Report. It makes no AI or machine learning claim anywhere on its public site. Its quality pages describe traceability, HACCP and ISO certification and residue analysis, and its hop variety and product pages are catalogue material, with no predictive model, sensory model or genomic breeding claim. Included because it is the largest single node in the hop supply chain and its lack of any public AI claim is itself a fact about the beverage data landscape.

Beer Marketer's Insights

beer · United States · Other · none · active beerinsights.com

no substantiated ai claim

Beer Marketer's Insights is the leading independent trade publisher of US beer market data, running for over 50 years on subscriber funding with no advertising. It publishes five subscription newsletters including the flagship Beer Marketer's INSIGHTS, Craft Brew News, Alcohol Issues Insights and Beverage Business Insights, plus the annual Beer Industry Update and two conferences a year. Its reporting reads shipment data from the Beer Institute and tracked off-premise scan channels, so it is a primary interpretation layer sitting on top of the syndicated measurement vendors. It makes no AI or machine learning claim; this is human analyst journalism over industry data.

Provenance: Scout: scout:marketdata. Last seen 2026-08-04.

  • http://www.beerinsights.com/
  • https://beerinsights.com/about-us

BeerRun (SWK Technologies)

beer · United States · Other · none · active beerrun.com

BeerRun is brewery management software for craft brewers, owned and sold by SWK Technologies, a US accounting and ERP reseller. It handles production logging with batch numbers, crew, date and tank capacity, a fermentation Gantt chart that reflows as batches move, and scheduling of future batches that are not committed until they hit the mash tun. It integrates with the brewery's accounting system so the reorder point is driven by projected brews against ingredients on hand rather than a static threshold, and it targets microbreweries in roughly the 1,000 to 50,000 barrel range. It has been on the market since at least 2013. The forecasting is deterministic projection arithmetic against planned batches, not machine learning, and no AI claim is made. Note that beerrun.com is a JavaScript single page app serving no crawlable text, so the product detail was verified on the owning vendor's own site.

Provenance: Scout: scout:erp. Last seen 2026-08-04.

  • https://www.beerrun.com/
  • https://www.swktech.com/what-is-beerrun/

Beerhive

beer · Belgium · Sensory & recipe · none · active beerhive.com

sensory data mining and beer recommendation

Beerhive, operated by Belgian company imkr BV in Leuven, runs a community beer tasting platform that scores beers against about 125 sensory flavour parameters rather than style categories, with roughly 10,600 beers and 2,170 breweries catalogued. It sells the resulting sensory data back to brewers as consumer perception insight, and offers venues a personalised digital beer advisor. The company describes itself as sensory data mining and offers preference based recommendations, but publishes no explicit machine learning claim and does not describe the underlying method. Founded by zythologists Jan Paesen and Wim De Bruycker and part of the Start it @KBC accelerator.

Provenance: Scout: scout:beer. Last seen 2026-08-01.

  • https://siliconcanals.com/news/startups/beerhive-this-belgium-app-will-suggest-a-beer-for-you-based-on-your-personality/
  • https://www.beerhive.com/

beer · Austria · Process & operations · none · active bertsch.at

no ai claim; brewhouse and process plant engineering

Bertsch Holding is an Austrian industrial group in Bludenz, Vorarlberg, founded in 1925, whose BERTSCHfoodtec division builds process plant and brewhouse systems for the food and beverage industry alongside energy and apparatus engineering. Verified via a browser pass after the site returned 403 to every non-browser client. Bevchek

beer · United States · Process & operations · none · active bevchek.com

draft beverage flow telemetry and pour-to-sale variance reconciliation

Bevchek sells flow sensors and a cloud platform that meter every ounce of draft beer, wine and cocktail poured in bars, taprooms, stadiums and chain venues, then reconcile pour data against POS sales nightly to expose variance, overpour and waste. It integrates with over 150 POS systems including Oracle MICROS, and adds temperature, pressure and foam alerts plus live keg depletion tracking. The company states it has metered over 17 billion pints across 21 countries. Bevchek makes no machine-learning claim on its own site: the product is sensor telemetry plus rule-based reconciliation and alerting, and the word AI appears only in a partner press release headline, not in any described capability.

Provenance: Scout: scout:oracle. Last seen 2026-07-19.

  • https://bevchek.com/about/

BeveragePro AI

beer · unknown · Process & operations · pilot · active beverageproai.com

AIoT monitoring of brewhouse, fermentation and keg assets

AIoT platform for beverage production plants covering brewhouse processing, fermentation management, blending, filtration, filling, canning, packaging and distribution. Breweries are the lead named segment, alongside soft drinks, water, juice, dairy and RTD, but not wine or spirits. The bulk of what is described is sensing rather than modelling: RFID and BLE for workforce and asset location, industrial sensors for fermentation temperature, tank level and humidity, GPS for tankers, over LoRaWAN and cellular. AI is claimed only in general terms as predictive analytics and AI forecasting models for consumption rates and equipment failure patterns, with no model detail given. Developed inside Aperture Venture Studio with support from GAO.

Provenance: Scout: scout:beer. Last seen 2026-08-04.

  • https://beverageproai.com/
  • https://beverageproai.com/breweries

BinaryBeer

beer · Australia · Consumer & personalization · shipping · active binarybeer.io

smart keg supply chain insights

BinaryBeer builds KegLink, a low power IoT device that turns a keg into a tracked container reporting location, temperature and beer freshness, plus the Binary Cloud platform that brewers use for fleet maps, alerts and reporting. The company states the platform is "powered by machine learning and AI-driven algorithms" that "continuously refine its insights" for fleet activity and supply chain optimisation, though it does not name the models or specific predictions. Asahi Europe and International selected BinaryBeer for an international smart keg deployment, and the product is also sold in North America through a partnership with Kegshoe.

Provenance: Scout: scout:logistics. Last seen 2026-08-01.

  • https://binarybeer.io/solution/platform/
  • https://www.craftbrewingbusiness.com/news/kegshoe-and-binary-beer-bring-new-keg-tracking-analytics-tech-to-north-america/

Block One Automation

beer · United States · Other · shipping · active blockone.com

warehouse execution orchestration for beverage dsd

Block One Automation sells a modular warehouse execution system called Pallet Factory built specifically for direct store delivery beverage distributors, combining a RoboArm palletizer, autonomous case-handling robots and very narrow aisle robotic forklifts, ERP integrated. Its own site markets the orchestration layer as smart AI software that makes real-time decisions, reroutes around issues and self-corrects exceptions such as a damaged case or a location mismatch. No model, training data or technique is named anywhere on the site, so the AI claim is marketing level rather than substantiated. Named beer distributor installations include Clark Distributing, Del Papa Distributing and King Beverage. Presented at the 2026 NBWA BIECC Innovation Showcase.

Provenance: Scout: scout:tradeshow_us. Last seen 2026-08-05.

BrauKon

beer · Germany · Sensory & recipe · none · active braukon.de

brewhouse process automation and recipe control

BrauKon is a Bavarian brewhouse and brewery plant OEM serving craft and mid-size breweries. It ships two named control products: BrauControl, a full brewery process automation system covering malt intake through to bright beer tank and CIP, with 60 recipe positions of 30 steps each, weekly brew scheduling, a brewing log and remote maintenance over a network connection, built almost entirely on Siemens components; and BrauKon TControl for brewhouse steps such as mashing, lautering and wort boiling, also available with remote maintenance. BrauControl includes self-optimising fuzzy logic lautering control that adjusts rake height, pump speed and sparging, which is classical control engineering and not machine learning. BrauKon makes no AI claim.

Provenance: Scout: scout:equipment. Last seen 2026-08-01.

  • https://braukon.de/en/produkt/braukon-braucontrol-en/
  • https://braukon.de/en/produkt/braukon-tcontrol-en/

Canadian brewery management software covering production scheduling, inventory, sales and distribution, keg tracking, compliance and payments, with QuickBooks Online and Square integrations. Named brewery customers include Rebellion Brewing, Pile O Bones Brewing and Split Rail Brewing. It makes no AI or machine learning claim anywhere on its site and is a straight system of record. It is included because it is the integration partner that feeds production data into Grist Analytics, so it matters to the beer data landscape.

BrewMan by Premier Systems

beer · United Kingdom · Other · active premiersystems.com

Premier Systems sells BrewMan, a cloud brewery and distillery management system used by over 300 producers globally, covering production and fluid movements, raw material traceability, cask and container tracking, dray planning and distribution, CRM, inventory and automatic UK alcohol duty calculation with audit reports. Variants are marketed for distilleries and wineries as well as breweries. The product is a system of record: nothing on the vendor site claims machine learning, and the only forward-looking feature is a rules-based forecast of stock required for upcoming production.

BrewTraq

beer · unknown · Other · none · active brewtraq.com

keg fleet tracking and delivery route optimisation

BrewTraq is a four-module brewery suite whose core product, Logistics, is keg tracking and delivery software for breweries and distributors. It covers barcode and QR keg scanning with full event history, multi-stop route building with vehicle compatibility checks and a mobile driver view, automatic deposit reconciliation, dwell tracking with account health scores, and reporting that includes demand forecasting, driver performance scorecards and seasonal demand patterns on the top tier. Pricing is public and starts at 79 US dollars a month. The site makes no machine learning claim: the forecasting and scoring features are described as reporting, not as models.

Provenance: Scout: scout:logistics. Last seen 2026-08-05.

BrewerSync

beer · Netherlands · Other · none · active brewersync.com

BrewerSync is a Dutch-built all-in-one management platform for craft breweries, sold on a freemium model with paid tiers at 49 and 99 euros per month. It handles orders and customer CRM, production and batch tracking, keg and cask tracking, delivery route planning, quality control logging, a customer self-service ordering portal, Moneybird accounting integration and Dutch excise duty filing. The site makes no AI or machine learning claim; it is a system of record with EU data hosting and GDPR positioning.

Brewers Association (Barley assistant)

beer · United States · Compliance & licensing · shipping · active brewersassociation.org

retrieval assistant over brewing technical and regulatory resources

The Brewers Association, the US craft brewing trade body, launched Barley in February 2026, a chat assistant that answers member questions about brewing techniques, industry data, legislative updates and regulatory guidance. It is deliberately constrained to Brewers Association content rather than the open web, and improves through thumbs up and down feedback from members. Technically this is a retrieval assistant over a curated corpus, not novel brewing ML, but it is a notable signal of institutional AI adoption in craft beer.

Provenance: Scout: scout:beer. Last seen 2026-07-26.

  • https://washingtonbeerblog.com/maintaining-the-human-touch-in-an-ai-driven-beer-world/
  • https://www.brewersassociation.org/association-news/meet-barley-your-new-guide-to-brewers-association-resources/

Brewers Dashboard

beer · unknown · Other · none · active brewersdashboard.com

Brewers Dashboard is a brewery data acquisition, automation and visualisation product built on a full SCADA software suite, sold to breweries from nano scale to large distributed facilities. It provides fermentation and brite tank temperature control, brewhouse monitoring and step mash programming, tank level, glycol, chiller and boiler monitoring, alarming to phones, and batch reports combining live temperature traces with laboratory results against the recipe. It is supplied by Infinite Automation Systems, which also does custom PLC and integration work. It makes no AI or machine learning claim; this is deterministic process control and reporting.

Provenance: Scout: scout:erp. Last seen 2026-08-01.

  • https://www.brewersdashboard.com/
  • https://www.brewersdashboard.com/how-it-works

Brewing-AI

beer · Switzerland · Sensory & recipe · pilot · active brewing-ai.com

recipe / flavor prediction

Brewing-AI, a solution from AlpVision SA, offers breweries a deep-reinforcement-learning system that combines operator settings with near-infrared spectroscopy and sensor data to recommend optimal fermentation parameters and hold beer quality steady across batches. It is sold as a process-optimization service to brewers.

Provenance: Hand-verified. Last seen 2026-07-21.

  • https://brewing-ai.com/

Cloud brewery management software covering production, sales and CRM, distribution, container tracking, a private online trade store and reporting, used by over 800 breweries. Built in the UK with explicit support for the Indian Rupee and local-language invoicing. BREWW LTD, incorporated 2018, Harpenden. Breww makes no public AI or machine-learning claim; it is tracked here as a brewery system of record, not as an AI vendor.

CDR (CDR BeerLab)

beer · Italy · Other · none · active cdrfoodlab.com

Hop Control

beer · Italy · Sensory & recipe · none · active hopcontrol.it

hop aroma profile prediction from essential oil composition

Hop Control is a hop aroma and dry hopping calculator built by the Italian craft brewery Birrificio Dada s.r.l. for breweries and homebrewers. It composes the essential oil profiles of every hop addition in a recipe, weighting them by boil time and dry hop contact, and outputs a predicted final aroma as a radar chart so brewers can model a hop schedule without trial brews. The database covers over 330 hop varieties with alpha and beta acids plus full aroma oil and sensory data, and the tool is available on web, iOS and Android for 19.99 euro per year after a seven day trial. The company is explicit that this is a deterministic algorithm grounded in published hop essential oil research and validated against test brews at Birrificio Dada, not machine learning; it makes no AI claim.

Provenance: Scout: scout:beer. Last seen 2026-08-05.

  • https://beersmith.com/blog/2024/10/23/the-hop-control-web-tool/
  • https://hopcontrol.it/

Steiner Hopfen GmbH, is a sixth generation global hop supplier of varieties, pellets and extracts, founded in 1845 and headquartered in Mainburg, Germany with US operations. Leuven Institute for Beer Research

beer · Belgium · Sensory & recipe · research · active libr.be

machine learning prediction of beer flavor and consumer appreciation

LIBR is an independent beer research institute embedded in KU Leuven that sells consultancy, R and D projects, pilot malting and two pilot breweries to the brewing industry, alongside genetics, microbial ecology and sensory analysis services. Its team, with the VIB-KU Leuven Center for Microbiology, measured over 200 chemical properties across 250 commercial Belgian beers in 22 styles, combined trained panel sensory data with more than 180,000 consumer reviews, and trained machine learning models that predict flavor and consumer appreciation and recommend aroma compounds to add. The models were validated by improving a commercial Belgian ale and an alcohol-free variant in blind tasting, published in Nature Communications in 2024. This is contract research and industry consultancy, not a productized software vendor.

The app carries the technician or pub cleaning schedule and transfers data from the beer-line cleaner into an encrypted customer database, so a brewer or pub company can confirm from head office that draught systems were cleaned correctly and on time. Spectramatics

beer · Denmark · Process & operations · shipping · active spectramatics.com

inline mid-infrared mash monitoring with chemometric models

Spectramatics sells SIBA, an in-line mid-infrared spectroscopy analyser that monitors mash and cereal cooking in a brewhouse in real time, reporting gelatinisation, sugar conversion and fermentability without manual sampling. The raw infrared spectra are interpreted by chemometric prediction models, and the EU OptiSpec grant that funded the work describes machine learning algorithms trained specifically for beer mashing. The company is the successor to Specshell, a 2014 Danish spin-out from DTU and MIT work that installed SIBA units in breweries in Europe, America and Africa before going bankrupt in 2022; Spectramatics took over the assets and IP and restarted the business with a new software package in 2023. A sister product, ZYMON, applies the same technique to bioethanol fermentation.

Provenance: Scout: scout:industrial. Last seen 2026-08-05.

Yakima Chief Hops

beer · United States · Supply chain · shipping · active yakimachief.com

predictive inventory modelling and brewing supply analytics

Yakima Chief Hops is a grower owned hop supplier selling pellets, Cryo products and extracts to brewers worldwide, and runs an extensive GC-QTOF and GC-SCD analytical and sensory programme to characterise beer soluble hop aroma compounds. A previous sweep rejected it for having no AI claim; the new evidence is a vendor case study describing its Business Technology team, an enterprise data warehouse with a dedicated data science sandbox, near real time BI across six business units and a model that predicts the probability of over and under inventory so overstock hops can be released for sale, targeted at a 5 to 10 percent revenue improvement. The verified modelling is commercial and supply analytics, not aroma or breeding prediction: the hop chemistry work is analytical chemistry with no machine learning claim attached. The corporate site returned HTTP 429 on repeated fetches during this sweep, so its own pages could not be quoted directly; the tools subdomain resolved.

Provenance: Scout: scout:ingredients. Last seen 2026-08-04.

  • https://baufest.com/en/yakima-chief-hops/
  • https://tools.yakimachief.com/
  • https://www.yakimachief.com/about-us/how-we-lead

beer · Italy · Process & operations · none · active yeastime.com

ultrasound fermentation acceleration with remote monitoring

Yeastime S.R.L. is an Italian fermentation hardware startup selling a non-invasive, retrofittable ultrasound device that clamps onto existing tanks and bioreactors, claiming up to 30 percent higher fermentation productivity with lower time, cost and energy. Yokogawa Electric

beer · Japan · Process & operations · none · active yokogawa.com

reinforcement-learning autonomous control of fermentation temperature scheduling

Japanese industrial automation and measurement group. Its autonomous control AI, Factorial Kernel Dynamic Policy Programming (FKDPP), was applied to beer fermentation in a 2025 proof of concept run by Yokogawa Digital with Craft Bank, a Kyoto Prefecture craft brewer: brewers manually implemented the AI-generated temperature setting schedule and cut fermentation from 336 hours to 240 hours, a 28 percent reduction, as stated in Yokogawa's own release. The AI claim is specific and quantified rather than promotional. Yokogawa also sells conventional beer fermentation instrumentation, including continuous monitoring by liquid density meter, which carries no ML claim. Recorded as pilot, not shipping: this is a published proof of concept, not a productised brewing offering.

forHops

A phone app tells Czech hop growers when, where and how much to irrigate, and estimates seasonal water requirement, expected yield and alpha acid content. The site states the predictive model runs in Microsoft Azure and uses machine learning methods over hundreds of millions of sensor readings from soil sensors, weather stations, satellite imagery and plant-mounted hop ECG sensors. Six farms were instrumented in autumn 2021, six more joined in 2024 for 28 fields, 40 further weather stations went in during spring 2025, and expansion to Slovakia is planned for 2026.

COLAClear

multiple · United States · Compliance & licensing · active colaclear.com

OCR label reading with a deterministic TTB compliance rules engine

COLAClear pre-screens US wine, spirits and beer labels against the federal and state rules that drive TTB COLA rejections, returning a colour-coded pass, review or fail report with Code of Federal Regulations citations in seconds. It runs over 30 checks covering class and type designation, alcohol content, standards of fill, the mandatory government health warning, sulfite declaration and grape variety, plus California and Oregon state rules. Public beta launched 4 May 2026 and is free during beta. On the AI question the vendor's own methodology page is explicit and conservative: label text is extracted by optical character recognition supplied by Google Cloud Vision, the checks are deterministic text-matching and pattern-validation against fixed regulatory requirements, and the page states the rule set does not rely on case law interpretation, regulatory inference or LLM-generated content. Launch press describing language-model reasoning is not supported by the vendor's own documentation. Founded by Zillah Bahar.

Provenance: Hand-verified. Last seen 2026-08-05.

  • https://colaclear.com/methodology.html
  • https://wineindustryadvisor.com/2026/05/04/colaclear-launches-public-beta-automated-ttb-label-pre-screen/

Figura Analytics

multiple · United Kingdom · Other · pilot · active figura-analytics.co.uk

particle fingerprinting of drinks samples with machine learning shape analysis

Figura Analytics is a Loughborough University spinout that built a patented nanopore flowcell using resistive pulse sensing to count and characterise every particle in a liquid sample in minutes, producing a fingerprint of what a normal sample should look like. Shape software is then applied to flag potential contaminants. Drinks analysis is one of its named target markets alongside healthcare, and co-founder Rhush Maugi is described as driving the software development and machine learning platform. Backed by SFC Capital, Innovate UK and angel investors since the January 2021 spinout. It exhibited at drinktec.

IES (Integrated Environmental Solutions)

multiple · United Kingdom · Process & operations · shipping · active iesve.com

distillery digital twin for energy and waste heat optimisation

IES builds physics-based digital twins of buildings and manufacturing sites. In a distillery project it pulled existing meter and process data into its iSCAN platform, used AI and machine learning to fill data gaps and disaggregate utility bills into granular time-series profiles, then simulated interventions in a 3D model of the plant. The twin quantified that waste heat recovery could meet 5 percent of heating demand and solar PV 4.1 percent of electricity, for 6.9 percent CO2 savings. Relevant to distilling decarbonisation, which is now a live pressure on Scotch producers.

Route optimisation software built only for beverage distributors running roughly ten to one hundred trucks, positioned explicitly against generic routing tools. The company frames beverage routing as a stop sequencing and three tier compliance constraint problem rather than a distance problem. Early stage: it is openly recruiting a founders cohort of five distributors, so the deployment base is unproven and HQ is not disclosed.

Polestar Analytics (Polestar Solutions)

multiple · United States · Demand & pricing · shipping · active polestaranalytics.com

trade promotion optimisation and demand forecasting for alcoholic beverages

Polestar Analytics is an analytics and planning consultancy with a named practice for alcoholic beverage companies, spanning beer, wine and spirits producers. For that vertical it builds market mix models, trade promotion optimisation, pricing analytics, sales and store optimisation, incentive compensation and supply chain control tower solutions. The methods it names are concrete rather than generic: non-linear response modelling, machine learning promotional attribution, ARIMA demand forecasting, Bayesian optimisation and Isolation Forest anomaly detection. It operates as Polestar Solutions and Services (India) and Polestar Insights (US), founded 2012 with offices across eight locations.

multiple · United States · Consumer & personalization · shipping · active prosperinsights.com

consumer alcohol propensity modeling

Prosper Insights & Analytics is a zero-party consumer data company that publishes pre-trained propensity models as Amazon SageMaker model packages on AWS Marketplace. Two of those listings are directly beverage-alcohol specific: Propensity-Drink Wine and Propensity-Drink Bourbon, which score the probability that a US adult drinks wine or bourbon from inputs such as gender, age range, household income and zip code. The wine model is trained on survey responses from over 17,000 US adults and reports a lift over random of 1.46. This is real supervised ML sold as a deployable model artifact, not a dashboard.

Coca-Cola runs Project Fizzion, a design intelligence system co-developed with Adobe and announced in May 2025. It encodes brand guidelines into a machine-readable StyleID that automatically applies brand rules inside Adobe Creative Cloud, built on Adobe Firefly generative AI, and is used to keep localized campaigns consistent across more than 200 markets. Coca-Cola states content production up to 10 times faster and describes Fizzion as still in pilot phase. The company also has a five-year cloud and generative AI partnership with Microsoft. The AI here is marketing and brand operations, not production or formulation.

AI4Whisky

whiskey · United Kingdom · Process & operations · research · active ai4whisky.com

carbon footprint modelling for single malt production

AI4Whisky is a free web tool from the International Centre for Brewing and Distilling at Heriot-Watt University that calculates the carbon footprint of single malt Scotch production across the distillery, maturation warehouse and bottling hall, covering Scope 1, 2 and 3 emissions including raw materials, transport and waste. A published case study on a 3,000 LPA batch distillery returned 2.83 kg CO2e per 70 cl bottle, with Scope 3 accounting for 77 percent. Despite the name it is a life cycle assessment calculator, not a machine learning model.

Arnold Holstein

whiskey · Germany · Other · none · active a-holstein.de

automated still control and fraction data acquisition

Arnold Holstein GmbH is a German still maker in Markdorf whose pot and fruit stills are used by distillers worldwide. Its automation option is an in-house control system operated from a stainless housed touch screen on the unit, regulating heating and distilling speed, dephlegmator target temperature, the separation of heads, hearts and tails, product pre-selection by fruit type and the rinsing cycle, with parameters saved and recalled for repeat batches. It also performs digital data acquisition of the concentrations and quantities of each fraction and offers connection to existing data acquisition systems. There is no named software brand, no remote access and no AI or machine learning claim anywhere in the offering: this is the plant layer that distillery process data comes from.

CannyCask

whiskey · United Kingdom · Other · shipping · active cannycask.io

cask ownership verification

CannyCask runs a whisky cask trading platform that verifies ownership of casks held in HMRC-regulated Scottish bonded warehouses. Its DAV VERIFIED mechanism cross-checks a claimed cask against warehouse custodial records and writes a dual-signed provenance record, with Polygon for the ledger, BitGo for custody, Onfido for identity checks and IPFS for document storage. At time of reading the site reported 22 verified casks worth over 99,000 pounds across two warehouses. There is no AI or machine learning in the product; it is cryptographic record keeping aimed at cask investment fraud.

Eluceda

whiskey · United Kingdom · Quality & inspection · shipping · active eluceda.com

spirits authentication from electrochemical fingerprints

Eluceda builds handheld point-of-use detection instruments for product authentication and chemical testing across spirits, pharmaceuticals, fuel and secure documents. Its E-Sens reader uses disposable catalytic electrodes that react with molecule groups in a liquid sample and produce a digital electrochemical signature in a few minutes. The signature is scored by algorithms against a reference database of authentic samples and returned as a percentage similarity, which the company says separates genuine whisky from counterfeits and from product cut with water, ethanol or other whiskies. The Scotch Whisky Research Institute supplied samples and consultancy for the whisky feasibility study. Eluceda describes algorithmic database matching rather than an explicit machine learning claim.

NABCA is the association of US alcohol control jurisdictions and the source of control state spirits sales and depletion data, covering roughly 25 percent of the distilled spirits market. Proof 8

whiskey · unknown · Process & operations · none · active proof-8.com

Proof 8, operated by CaskStore AG, sells an operating system for distilleries and bonded warehouses covering production tracking, maturation monitoring, bottling and HMRC and TTB compliance. Reactory

whiskey · New Zealand · Other · none · active reactory.tech

accelerated spirit maturation

Reactory builds reactor hardware that replicates the spirit, oak and environmental chemistry of barrel ageing, claiming a ten year equivalent spirit in 72 hours at batch sizes from 2 litres to 20,000 litres and up to a 55 percent cut in cost of goods per bottle. It went from New Zealand to global availability in 2026, and a rum matured with the technology, Queen of Clubs, took gold at the 2026 London Spirits Competition. The company makes no AI or machine learning claim; the approach is chemistry and precision process control. It matters to this landscape as a process-data alternative to cask time.

SmartCask by Checkpoint

whiskey · unknown · Demand & pricing · shipping · active smartcask.app

cask digital twin with rag assistant and maturation forecasting

SmartCask is a dual NFC and RFID smart tag system from Checkpoint Systems, part of CCL Industries, that creates linked digital twins for every spirit cask. Each cask carries a Cask Twin holding cooperage origin, wood type, repairs and ownership changes, and a Fill Twin holding distillation date, maturation conditions and bottling records, with optional blockchain anchoring for high value casks. The underlying SmartLinks platform is where the AI sits: the site advertises an Ask Your Cask conversational assistant described as RAG powered, plus AI-native intelligence that surfaces portfolio insights and 20 year runway forecasting, angel's share modelling with exponential decay, cohort analysis and release readiness planning. It was launched into the Scotch cask investment market in early 2026 as a traceability and fraud prevention answer after the BBC Disclosure programme on cask fraud.

barrel maturation analytics and angel's share forecasting

Spirit Sight is barrel management and distillery operations software for American whiskey producers, tracking each cask by serial, lot, mashbill, fill and dump date, entry and current proof, volume, location and live value. Its differentiator is a live 3D rickhouse view with gap detection and temperature and humidity heatmaps, plus per-barrel evaporative loss tracking that turns angel's share into a forecastable cost feeding valuation and TTB reporting. The analytics are statistical and sensor driven rather than machine learning.

iStill B.V. builds modular automated mashing, fermentation and distillation equipment for craft distilleries and says it serves more than 1,500 distilleries worldwide. Its stills run what the company calls level three automation and robotization: sensors drive the cuts between heads, hearts and tails, reflux and air pressure compensation are handled by the controller, and validated recipes replay with no operator intervention. That control layer is deterministic process logic, not machine learning, and iStill does not claim otherwise. Separately the company has added DistillingAI to its website, a generative assistant it describes as the easiest way to learn about everything related to distilling and iStill, which is a customer facing language model rather than anything running on the still.

Aiiro Systems Inc. (Aiiro Systems Kabushiki Kaisha, 藍色システムズ株式会社) operates Sakenowa (さけのわ), a Japanese sake check-in app and open data service running since 2013. Its own about page states that the Flavor Chart (registered Japanese trademark 6520969) and the Flavor Search feature are produced by a computer program that analyses posted user comments using natural language processing, then aggregates the result into per-brand flavour scores. The company publishes the derived data free as the Sakenowa Data HTTP API, exposing six-axis flavour vectors, flavour tags, and brand, brewery and ranking endpoints for third parties to build on. Incorporated 2019-06-10 in Kudanminami, Chiyoda, Tokyo; the service predates the company.

Atol CD (VineMapper)

wine · France · Agriculture & crop · shipping · active atolcd.com

tractor mounted computer vision for vine disease mapping

Atol CD is a French digital services firm with offices in Paris, Dijon, Lyon, Besancon and Gevrey-Chambertin that has worked with the agricultural sector for over 25 years. On 30 October 2025 it acquired VineMapper from the failed startup Greenshield. VineMapper is a high definition camera with embedded AI mounted on a tractor or straddle machine that identifies vine anomalies, principally early downy mildew symptoms, and maps them at centimetre level positioning with photographic evidence. Atol CD says it will add a new data processing platform and API integration into existing farm management systems.

Avterra

vineyard sensor data integration and irrigation decision guidance

Avterra is a small vineyard technology consultancy on California's Central Coast that integrates data from existing or newly installed telemetry and sensor devices into a single portal, then advises on when and how much to irrigate. It positions itself explicitly as vendor neutral, pulling from a multitude of telemetry devices rather than selling one stack, and leans on over 30 years of vineyard management and irrigation consulting experience. The guidance is human expert interpretation of integrated data, not an algorithm. It exhibited at WIN Expo Sonoma at booth 431.

BioScout

wine · Australia · Agriculture & crop · shipping · active bioscout.com.au

airborne spore detection for vineyard disease risk

BioScout makes an autonomous solar powered air sampler that captures airborne fungal spores and images them by light microscopy at 5K resolution, down to 2 microns. Machine vision models classify the spores and return grower facing disease pressure data within about 24 hours, which vineyards use to decide when to spray for downy mildew, powdery mildew and botrytis. Wine industry partners named on its own site include Treasury Wine Estates and Mollydooker. The sensor is also sold in France, priced in French trade press at about 7,000 euros a year.

Provenance: Scout: scout:wine. Last seen 2026-08-04.

Bodega Anaia

wine · Argentina · Agriculture & crop · none · active anaiawines.com

connected vineyard sensing and precision viticulture

Bodega Anaia is a 72 hectare winery and wine tourism estate in Agrelo, Lujan de Cuyo, Mendoza. Argentine business press describes it as one of the most advanced connected vineyard implementations in the region: IoT sensors reading soil moisture, temperature and mineral presence, transmitted over LoRa to the cloud and controlled from a mobile app, combined with satellite monitoring to detect small variations in soil composition and target water and fertiliser per block. The winery's own website covers the estate, wines and lodge only and makes no technology, sensor or AI claim, so the sensing programme is established from press reporting rather than from the operator itself. Bodegas Cepa 21

wine · Spain · Other · pilot · dormant cepa21.com

harvest and vintage decision support

Ribera del Duero winery of the Emilio Moro family that commissioned a bespoke AI system to inform winemaking decisions each harvest. The system, built with IBM and consultancy Integra using Watson Studio, Watson Machine Learning and Cognos Analytics, cross-references historical winemaking data held by the Ribera del Duero Designation of Origin regulatory board with historical weather data from The Weather Company. Reported outputs are viticultural zoning of the region, links between climatic events and grape parameters, and prediction of grape ripening indices. The AI is not built in house; the winery is the operator of a system built for it, and the most recent public evidence of the project dates from August 2020.

Bragato Research Institute

wine · New Zealand · Quality & inspection · research · active bri.co.nz

predicting wine quality from wine chemistry

Bragato Research Institute is the research arm of the New Zealand wine industry, wholly owned by New Zealand Winegrowers and set up in 2015 as the country's first Regional Research Institute. Its Pinot Noir programme applies machine learning to predict relationships between wine chemistry and perceptions of wine quality. Other data work includes grapevine genomic sequencing for the Sauvignon Blanc 2.0 clone programme, 5 km granularity climate change projections for all nine New Zealand wine regions using NIWA data, and an industry-wide research data catalogue and structured database.

Bucher Vaslin

wine · France · Other · none · active buchervaslin.com

connected press monitoring and remote fault finding

Bucher Vaslin is a French winery plant OEM best known for its Bucher XPlus and XPert pneumatic grape presses, and part of the Swiss Bucher Industries group. Its presses ship with Winect, a smartphone application that shows the pressing programme in progress, time remaining, pressure and flow rate, plus configurable email and SMS alerts for events such as end of pressing, juice selection and press stop, and a remote assistance path so a technician can fault find without a site visit. Operating data is uploaded to a Bucher cloud through the company Connect portal for follow up and anomaly notification. Bucher Vaslin makes no AI or machine learning claim: this is connected telemetry and remote service, and it matters because it is where a large share of primary winemaking process data originates.

Cropsy Technologies

wine · New Zealand · Agriculture & crop · shipping · active cropsy.tech

vine-by-vine computer vision scanning for disease, yield and pruning counts

Cropsy mounts camera scanners on tractors, harvesters and ATVs that profile individual grapevines as the vehicle drives through the block, up to 8,000 vines per scanner per hour. On-board AI tags each vine and returns disease detection, cane and bud counts, missing-vine maps and yield estimates through its Osiris software. This is genuine computer vision inference running at the edge, not just sensor logging. Customers named on its own site include Cloudy Bay and Martell.

Deep Sky Vineyard

wine · United States · Agriculture & crop · none · active deepskyvineyard.com

sensor driven precision irrigation and vineyard analytics

Family owned Arizona winery with vineyards in Arizona and Argentina that co-developed a smart vineyard platform with Google Cloud Technology Partner niolabs. Sensors across the blocks feed water flow and soil moisture readings through Cloud IoT Core and Pub/Sub into BigQuery, with Looker Studio for analysis, letting the growers irrigate by gallons per vine rather than by timer. Published results include roughly 1.6 million gallons of water saved in 2018 and the elimination of shrivel and bunch rot in their Grenache. The company makes no machine learning claim: the Google Cloud case study says only that it is exploring ML for future yield insight, and its own technology page describes zone profiling and record keeping rather than models.

wine · France · Agriculture & crop · shipping · active fruitionsciences.com

vine water status and grape maturation prediction from sap flow and climate data

Fruition Sciences runs a vineyard monitoring platform that links climate, irrigation, soil maps, fertilisation and plant development data to fruit maturation, delivered as cloud and mobile software for winemakers. In-vine sap flow sensors report every 15 minutes and proprietary algorithms compute reference evapotranspiration, plant transpiration and vine stress. The company states on its own site that it applies machine learning techniques alongside plant physiology and GIS expertise to generate predictive alerts. Montpellier is the French base, with a second office in Napa, California.

Provenance: Scout: scout:wine. Last seen 2026-07-26.

  • https://fruitionsciences.com/en/sap-flow-irrigation-sensors

wine · unknown · Agriculture & crop · pilot · active integrainnovation.com

viticultural zoning and grape ripening prediction

Spanish technology and strategy consultancy with a Food and Beverages practice. Integrape

wine · New Zealand · Agriculture & crop · pilot · active integrape.com

vine level data platform with machine learning disease detection

Integrape runs a viticulture data platform that aggregates lab results, winery data and historical vineyard records into vine level spatial records, plus VineAudit for per vine and per gap mapping and the Vure mobile app for field data capture. The platform itself is a system of record and analytics rather than an in house machine learning product. The machine learning component, VinEye, is an algorithm developed by New Zealand's Plant and Food Research that identifies grapevine leafroll disease from photographs of vines; Integrape has been integrating it with its platform and running robot mounted camera surveys in Hawke's Bay and Marlborough with partners Agri Automation and Bitwise Agronomy. Integrape and Vure are used on roughly a third of New Zealand's planted vineyard area, more than 300 vineyards.

IntelliCulture

wine · United States · Agriculture & crop · none · active intelliculture.com

vineyard equipment telematics and spray pass verification

IntelliCulture is a GPS and farm management platform purpose built for specialty crop farms including vineyards, connecting existing GPS hardware or its own. It tracks what equipment is actually doing in the block rather than just where it is, verifying every spray pass, computing true cost per acre, automating OSHA inspection records and GHG reporting. The company says it covers 750,000 acres of nut, vineyard, orchard and specialty crop farms. The analytics are telematics derived; no machine learning claim is made. International Wineries for Climate Action

wine · unknown · Supply chain · none · active iwcawine.org

winery greenhouse gas inventory and carbon accounting

IWCA is a working group of wineries founded in 2019 by Familia Torres of Penedes, Spain and Jackson Family Wines of California, which has developed carbon accounting methodologies specific to the wine sector and requires members to produce verified greenhouse gas inventories. It is a partner of the United Nations Race to Zero campaign, and named members include Yealands Estate Wines in Australia, VSPT Wine Group in Chile, Neleman in Spain, Voyager Estate in Australia and Sogrape in Portugal. Its value to the beverage data landscape is the protocol and the verified emissions data set it forces members to build; it makes no AI or machine learning claim.

Parsec

wine · Italy · Process & operations · none · active parsecsrl.net

algorithmic fermentation kinetics control and nutrient dosing

Parsec builds automation and control systems for wineries, covering micro-oxygenation, fermentation control, selective extraction and real-time monitoring, with patented technology installed in more than 30 countries. Its named products are the SAEn5000 monitoring and vinification automation platform, ADCF (dynamic analysis of fermentation kinetics), which measures CO2 output continuously to track sugar consumption and flag slowdowns or stuck fermentations, EVO2FERM, which adapts yeast nutrient additions in real time, and Aphromate for sparkling wine prise de mousse. Parsec describes these as intelligent algorithms developed experimentally; there is no machine learning claim. Parsec is being acquired by Enartis.

Peritos Solutions

wine · New Zealand · Other · shipping · active peritossolutions.com

wine label recognition and provenance enrichment on aws

Peritos Solutions is a cloud, data and AI consultancy operating across India, New Zealand, Australia and the United States, and an AWS partner. For Wine-Searcher it built an AI label assist pipeline: a custom CNN trained on more than 500,000 labelled wine label images hosted on an Amazon SageMaker real-time endpoint, Amazon Textract OCR to structure label text into JSON, and RAG-grounded provenance summaries generated with Amazon Bedrock against a wine knowledge base indexed in OpenSearch. Its own case page claims 94 percent label identification accuracy, enrichment of over 8 million wine listings, and a drop from 15 to 20 minutes of manual research per SKU to under 2 seconds. AWS separately published a Wine-Searcher and Peritos success story in August 2025 covering the wider AWS platform work.

Selartag

wine · France · Demand & pricing · shipping · active selartag.com

wine cellar inventory and sales advisory

Selartag is a French startup that digitises wine cellar and bottle inventory using RFID or QR smart labels, handheld and fixed portal readers, and a mobile and web app, sold to restaurants, sommeliers, wine merchants, collectors and vineyards. Its AWS architecture is documented in an AWS partner success story built with Eviden/Atos, running Lambda, EC2, RDS Postgres and Amazon Bedrock. Bedrock powers the AI features: auto-generating bottle records in one click, suggesting which wines are likely to sell based on historical data and weather, and food pairing recommendations. The case study reports inventory audits dropping from over two days to under 30 minutes and error rates from roughly 10 percent to under 1 percent.

Solumatic (Vitisoft / VINCA)

wine · France · Process & operations · shipping · active vitisoft.fr

conversational assistant over winery management data

Solumatic publishes Vitisoft, a management suite used by more than 1,600 wine and spirits businesses in France covering CRM, marketing, stock and bottling, invoicing, point of sale and DRM/DAES excise compliance. Its own site lists an integrated AI called VINCA under decision statistics. French trade press reports VINCA shipped in the second half of 2025 and is built on Mistral models, answering plain language questions about the estate's own Vitisoft data and drafting commercial prospecting messages. The rest of the suite is a system of record rather than machine learning.

TerraNIS (Oenoview)

wine · France · Agriculture & crop · shipping · active terranis.fr

satellite mapping for precision viticulture

TerraNIS is a French Earth observation SME founded in March 2014 by two former Airbus/Spot Image space applications specialists. Its wine product Oenoview, co-developed and sold with the oenological consultancy Groupe ICV, turns high resolution satellite imagery into vineyard maps used for harvest planning and lot allocation, fertilisation, vine water status and young vine growth checks. Processing rests on in-house remote sensing algorithms; the company describes using an artificial intelligence approach in specific work such as mapping biodiversity features in Champagne vineyards, rather than as the core of the product. Around 20 staff, roughly a quarter of revenue in R&D, with a subsidiary in Chile.

Provenance: Scout: scout:wine. Last seen 2026-08-05.

  • https://www.terranis.fr/en/about-us/
  • https://www.terranis.fr/viticulture-oad-oenoview/

Terraview

wine · Switzerland · Agriculture & crop · shipping · active terraview.gmbh

vineyard yield, risk and irrigation forecasting

Terraview builds TerraviewOS, a vineyard management platform that merges satellite and field data into ripening analysis, production controls and block level decision support for wine grape growers. The company markets it plainly as an AI powered solution to raise yield and cut costs, with production, risk and irrigation forecasts. In July 2024 Terraview merged with the Swiss hyperspectral crop analytics company Gamaya and now operates from Lausanne; Yury Vasilkov is CEO and Terraview founder Piyush Harsh is Chief Science Officer. Named users include Vallformosa in Spain.

Provenance: Scout: scout:wine. Last seen 2026-08-01.

The Australian Wine Research Institute (AWRI)

wine · Australia · Other · active awri.com.au

no substantiated ml claim; wine analytical chemistry and genomics data

The AWRI is the Australian wine industry's research, development and extension body, based at the Waite campus in South Australia. It runs Metabolomics SA for analytical chemistry profiling, the South Australian Genomics Centre for genetic analysis, a wine microorganism culture collection and a trial-scale no- and low-alcohol facility, and provides grapevine clonal identification services. It is a primary generator of wine composition and sensory data for the industry, but its own site does not substantiate a machine learning or predictive modelling claim, so it is recorded here as data infrastructure rather than an AI vendor.

Provenance: Scout: scout:wine. Last seen 2026-08-05.

Unhyped AI (Vintelligence)

wine · Singapore · Agriculture & crop · shipping · active vintelligence.dev

terroir and vineyard parcel intelligence API

Unhyped AI runs a spatial intelligence engine over roughly 7.3 million land parcels, scoring more than 800,000 vineyard plots on bedrock geology, slope, elevation, aspect and classification across 19 to 23 regions in France, Spain, Italy, Germany and Australia. It sells the data two ways: Vintelligence, a REST and MCP native API for wine businesses and AI assistants, and Unhyped, a consumer app that surfaces bottles sharing terroir with Grand Cru sites at lower prices. The engine is geological and geospatial scoring plus producer lineage mapping rather than a learned taste model, and the company says so on its own site.

Provenance: Scout: scout:wine. Last seen 2026-08-04.

  • https://vintelligence.dev/
  • https://www.unhypedwine.com/

Vigneto Sicuro (Trace Technologies)

wine · Italy · Agriculture & crop · shipping · active vignetosicuro.it

vineyard disease and weather risk prediction

Vigneto Sicuro is a subscription app for winegrowers built by Trace Technologies S.r.l., an Abruzzo startup. It pulls real-time climate data for a vineyard block selected on a map, with no hardware installed in the field, and runs prediction models the company describes as artificial intelligence to produce a downy mildew (peronospora) and powdery mildew (oidio) risk index several days ahead, plus a localised weather forecast. Products are sold as Peronosporazero, Oidiozero and Meteowine. The claimed 99 percent accuracy figure is the vendor's own marketing and is not independently substantiated.

Vina Concha y Toro

wine · Chile · Process & operations · pilot · active vinacyt.com

harvest volume prediction from drone and sensor data

Vina Concha y Toro is one of the largest wine producers in South America and runs its own Center for Research and Innovation, opened in 2014 in Chile's Maule Region. The CRI has built an artificial intelligence model that combines multispectral drone imagery with terrain data, weather stations and ground sensors to predict harvest volume; in reported trials the model gave prediction errors of 2 to 9 percent against 20 to 30 percent for traditional estimation methods. The company also cites AI for irrigation scheduling and for diagnosing fungal disease from tractor mounted cameras. This is an operator, not a vendor: the models are its own R and D, developed with the Chilean partner Smartdici and public funding from Corfo.

Provenance: Scout: scout:wine. Last seen 2026-05-01.

  • https://vinacyt.com/en/corporate-pillars/innovation/
  • https://vinacyt.com/en/noticia/innovation/artificial-intelligence-and-drones-new-harvest-volume-prediction-system/

Vineyard Robotics (Orchard Robotics)

wine · United States · Agriculture & crop · shipping · active vineyard-robotics.com

computer vision yield estimation per vine

Vineyard Robotics is the vineyard division of Orchard Robotics, which describes itself as an AI farming company. Its FruitScope Vision camera system mounts to a tractor or ATV and is driven down vineyard rows at 1 to 12 mph, using computer vision models to count and size buds, shoots, flowers and clusters on every individual vine. Data lands in FruitScope Vault, a system of record, and FruitScope OS, a vineyard management software suite. The company states it has built large multi-season crop datasets and is developing Canary, an AI decision layer that is not yet released.

Vinicola Casa Tertulia

wine · Brazil · Sensory & recipe · shipping · active vinicolacasatertulia.com.br

genetic algorithms and machine learning for wine blend composition

Casa Tertulia is a family winery in the Alto Uruguai region of Rio Grande do Sul that built its own blending system using machine learning and genetic algorithms. The system reads the physicochemical structure of each lot, learns from the scores that professional analysts gave internationally awarded wines, and searches blend combinations that could not be tasted by hand, predicting how a candidate blend will be rated in sensory analysis. The output is a commercial product line: Corte I, Genetic Algorithm No 10, a blend of Marselan, Merlot, Teroldego and Cabernet Sauvignon, presented at ProWine Sao Paulo 2024. The AI is the winery's own work, developed in-house by algorithm engineer Gabriel Hilgert alongside enologist Viviane Hilgert.

Provenance: Scout: scout:aws. Last seen 2026-08-01.

  • https://prowinesaopaulo.com/noticias/prowine-sao-paulo-recebe-primeiro-vinho-elaborado-com-inteligencia-artificial-casa-tertulia-lanca-tecnologia-brasileira-inedita-para-elaboracao-de-vinhos-de-corte/
  • https://vinicolacasatertulia.com.br/ia/

VinoCheck

wine · Canada · Consumer & personalization · none · active vinocheck.com

direct to consumer wine compliance and tax filing automation

VinoCheck is wine compliance software for wineries selling direct to consumer. It calculates destination based sales tax, wine specific fees and pass through taxes on orders, runs real time compliance checks against volume limits, dry areas and ABV restrictions, and generates sales tax, excise tax and direct shipper filings in one click. It offers a free backtest against a winery's historical order data. This is rules driven compliance automation with no AI claim. It exhibited at WIN Expo Sonoma at booth 342.

Vinventions

wine · Belgium · Other · shipping · active vinventions.com

wine oxygen and phenolic measurement instruments

Vinventions makes wine closures (NOMACORC synthetic, SUBR micro natural cork, VINTOP screwcaps) and, through its Wine Quality Solutions division, analytical instruments used by winemakers: Nomasense O2 and P6000 oxygen meters, Nomasense CO2, Polyscan for phenolic and oxidation potential measurement, and colour analysis tools. It makes no artificial intelligence or machine learning claim; WQS is measurement hardware and decision support based on established oenological chemistry. It matters to the wine data landscape as one of the main sources of in cellar oxygen and phenolic measurement data. The company was formed in 2015 around Nomacorc, founded by the Belgian Noel family, and is headquartered in Wallonia.

Weather Trends International (weathertrends360)

wine · unknown · Demand & pricing · shipping · active weathertrends360.com

year ahead weather forecasting for wine shipping and demand planning

Weather Trends International sells year ahead weekly temperature and precipitation forecasts and weather driven sales analytics, delivered as datasets on AWS Data Exchange and AWS Marketplace under the weathertrends360 brand. Its named beverage customer is the direct to consumer wine retailer Wine Access, which subscribes to the Daily 14-Day Forecast dataset and uses it to decide when to place weather holds on wine shipments so bottles are not left in heat or freezing conditions, documented in an AWS Marketplace blog post. The forecasting method the company describes is statistical, built on 100 plus years of weather history and 24 oceanic climate cycles; the site makes no machine learning or AI claim.

Wine Opinions

wine · unknown · Other · none · active wineopinions.com

no substantiated ai claim

Wine Opinions provides US wine market research to wineries, wine marketing companies and trade associations, using proprietary national panels of more than 4,000 US wine trade members and over 14,000 US wine consumers weighted to high frequency and high end buyers. Services are quantitative consumer and trade surveys, qualitative focus groups and moderated tastings, and baseline and tracking studies. It is a wine only consumer intelligence house rather than a scan data reseller. The site makes no AI or machine learning claim; the method is panel survey research analysed by its own team.

wine · France · Demand & pricing · shipping · active wine-services.com

wine and spirits market, pricing and distribution intelligence

Wine Services is a Bordeaux-based market intelligence platform tracking on-trade and off-trade distribution, pricing and competitive placement for wine and spirits brands. Wine Technology Incorporated (VinWizard)

wine · United States · Process & operations · none · active vinwizard.com

winery tank and cellar process control

Wine Technology Incorporated sells VinWizard, a winery automation, monitoring and control system used by over 200 wineries, with a second base in Marlborough, New Zealand. It controls tank temperature, automated pumpovers, air injection, headspace gassing and agitators, reads in-tank sensors for temperature stratification, brix and redox, and extends to chillers, barrel and case goods rooms, CO2, water and power. Its IOWIZ-4 and IOWIZ-16 IO controllers have been built in-house since 2016, and CellarView is a browser-based digital tank board. Alerts go out by email or SMS when tanks drift out of range. No AI or machine learning claim is made.

eProvenance

wine · unknown · Other · none · active eprovenance.com

wine cold chain scoring

eProvenance is a Franco American company that monitors the temperature and shipping conditions of fine wine at container, pallet or case level, using sensors placed in shipments and analysis of the resulting condition data. Its proprietary scientific algorithm converts a shipment temperature history into the eProvenance Score, a 0 to 100 rating of whether the wine stayed fresh or was heat damaged. In 2020 it launched VinAssure, a wine supply chain platform built on IBM Blockchain Transparent Supply. The scoring algorithm is described as scientific and rule based rather than machine learning, and the company makes no explicit ML claim.

Provenance: Scout: scout:logistics. Last seen 2026-08-01.

vinSUITE

wine · United States · Other · shipping · active vinsuite.com

wine club member churn prediction

vinSUITE is a direct-to-consumer winery platform covering wine club management, tasting room POS, ecommerce, CRM and payments, claiming over 200,000 wine club members supported and more than 2 billion dollars in annual DTC sales processed. Its vinSIGHT analytics module is the AI component: a churn model that scores which club members are about to cancel using signals such as skipped shipments, fewer visits and declining engagement, then surfaces a weekly at-risk list with recommended actions. The company advertises 94 percent prediction confidence and positions the churn model as the one thing competing DTC platforms do not offer; the confidence figure is a vendor claim with no published methodology.
